text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/lib/assignWork.ts\n@@\n import { resolveConditionalAssignment, type ConditionalAssignmentTarget } from '@/lib/conditionalBootcampAssignment';\n+import { assignReadingCenter, READING_CENTER_PATH } from '@/lib/readingCenterAccess';\n@@\n   | { kind: 'activity'; activityId: string }\n+  | { kind: 'reading_center' }\n@@\n   switch (item.kind) {\n+    case 'reading_center':\n+      throw new Error('Assign Reading Center separately so its access grant can be verified.');\n@@\n }): Promise<AssignWorkResult> {\n+  if (input.item.kind === 'reading_center') {\n+    const ok: StudentResult[] = [];\n+    const failed: StudentResult[] = [];\n+    for (const studentId of [...new Set(input.studentIds)]) {\n+      try {\n+        const { item, reused } = await assignReadingCenter({\n+          studentId, scheduledForLocalDate: input.scheduledForLocalDate,\n+          title: input.overrides?.title, description: input.overrides?.description,\n+        });\n+        // Repeating a successful assignment returns its existing launcher and\n+        // does not send the same notification again.\n+        let notify: NotifyResult | undefined;\n+        if (input.notify !== false && !reused) {\n+          try {\n+            notify = await notifyStudentOfClassroomItem({ adminUserId: input.teacherId, studentId,\n+              title: item.title, description: item.description ?? undefined,\n+              route: READING_CENTER_PATH, assignmentType: 'reading practice' });\n+          } catch (error) {\n+            console.error('[assignWork] Reading Center notification failed after verified assignment', error);\n+            notify = { inbox_ok: false, bell_ok: false, email_ok: false };\n+          }\n+        }\n+        ok.push({ studentId, assignmentId: item.id, classroomItemId: item.id,\n+          route: READING_CENTER_PATH, classroomItemIds: [item.id], notify });\n+      } catch (error) {\n+        failed.push({ studentId, error: message(error, 'Could not assign Reading Center') });\n+      }\n+    }\n+    return { ok, failed };\n+  }\n   return runAssign({ ...input, items: [input.item] });\n }\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/lib/classroomItems.ts\n@@\n import type { ClassroomItem } from '@/hooks/useClassroomItems';\n+import { isReadingCenterAssignment, READING_CENTER_PATH } from '@/lib/readingCenterAccess';\n@@\n export function resolveItemRoute(item: ClassroomItem): string | null {\n+  if (isReadingCenterAssignment(item)) return READING_CENTER_PATH;\n*** End Patch"));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 10.8 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c990e5","wall_time_seconds":0.4738084,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":427,"output":"The application of the rational model to the house-building process in the 1950s and ’60s led to suburban communities consisting of nearly identical structures. Indeed, it was possible to wander into the residence of someone else and not to realize immediately that one was not at home. The more expensive developments were superficially more diversified, but their interior layouts assumed residents who were indistinguishable in their requirements. Furthermore, the planned communities themselves look very similar. Established trees are bulldozed to facilitate construction. In their place, a number of saplings, held up by posts and wire, are planted. Streets are laid out in symmetrical grid patterns. With such uniformity, suburbanites may well enter the wrong subdivision or become lost in their own.\\r\\n\\r\\nMany of Steven Spielberg’s films are set in such suburbs. Spielberg’s strategy is to lure the viewer into this highly repetitive world and then to have a completely unexpected event occur. For example, the film Poltergeist takes place in a conventional suburban household in which evil spirits ultimately disrupt the sameness. (The spirits first manifest themselves through another key element of the homogeneous society—the television set.) The great success of Spielberg’s films may be traceable to a longing for some unpredictability, even if it is bizarre and menacing, in increasingly routinized lives.$rc$,\\r\\n  0,\\r\\n  1\\r\\n)\\r\\non conflict (slug) do nothing;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- word_count for the seed: count whitespace-separated words.\\r\\nupdate public.reading_center_passages\\r\\nset word_count = array_length(regexp_split_to_array(trim(body), \'\\\\s+\'), 1)\\r\\nwhere slug = \'living-in-a-rational-society\';\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/pages/teacher/tools/TeacherReadingCenter.tsx\n+import { useState } from 'react';\n+import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';\n+import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';\n+import { useAuth } from '@/contexts/AuthContext';\n+import { useHubStudents } from '@/hooks/useHubStudents';\n+import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\n+import { assignWork } from '@/lib/assignWork';\n+import { revokeReadingCenter, READING_CENTER_PATH } from '@/lib/readingCenterAccess';\n+import type { ClassroomItem } from '@/hooks/useClassroomItems';\n+\n+export default function TeacherReadingCenter() {\n+  const { user } = useAuth();\n+  const { students, loading: studentsLoading, error: studentsError } = useHubStudents();\n+  const [params] = useSearchParams();\n+  const [studentId, setStudentId] = useState(params.get('student') ?? '');\n+  const [scheduled, setScheduled] = useState('');\n+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n+  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState('');\n+  const cache = useQueryClient();\n+  const assignments = useQuery({\n+    queryKey: ['reading-center-assignment-admin', studentId], enabled: !!studentId,\n+    queryFn: async () => {\n+      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('classroom_items').select('*')\n+        .eq('student_id', studentId).eq('source_table', 'reading_center').eq('source_id', 'library')\n+        .eq('visible_to_student', true).in('status', ['assigned', 'in_progress', 'completed']);\n+      if (error) throw error;\n+      return (data ?? []) as ClassroomItem[];\n+    },\n+  });\n+  const current = assignments.data?.[0];\n+  const refresh = async () => {\n+    await Promise.all([\n+      cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['reading-center-assignment-admin', studentId] }),\n+      cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['classroom-items-admin', studentId] }),\n+      cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['classroom-items', studentId] }),\n+      cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['reading-center-access', studentId] }),\n+    ]);\n+  };\n+  const assign = async () => {\n+    if (!user || !studentId) return;\n+    setBusy(true); setFeedback('');\n+    try {\n+      const result = await assignWork({ teacherId: user.id, studentIds: [studentId], item: { kind: 'reading_center' },\n+        scheduledForLocalDate: scheduled || undefined });\n+      if (result.failed.length) throw new Error(result.failed[0].error);\n+      await refresh();\n+      setFeedback(result.ok[0]?.notify && !Object.values(result.ok[0].notify).every(Boolean)\n+        ? 'Reading Center is assigned and visible in the student’s work. A notification did not arrive.'\n+        : 'Reading Center assignment verified. The student can open it from their assigned work.');\n+    } catch (error) { setFeedback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Assignment failed.'); }\n+    finally { setBusy(false); }\n+  };\n+  const revoke = async () => {\n+    setBusy(true); setFeedback('');\n+    try { await revokeReadingCenter(studentId); await refresh(); setFeedback('Reading Center access removed. Saved reading work is preserved.'); }\n+    catch (error) { setFeedback(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not remove access.'); }\n+    finally { setBusy(false); }\n+  };\n+\n+  return <div className=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-6 px-5 py-6 sm:px-6\">\n+    <div><h1 className=\"text-2xl font-semibold\">Assign Reading Center</h1>\n+      <p className=\"mt-2 text-sm text-black/65\">Give a student access to the reading tool. It appears in their assigned work when its start date arrives.</p></div>\n+    <Link className=\"inline-block rounded border border-black/20 px-4 py-2 text-sm\" to={READING_CENTER_PATH}>Open Reading Center</Link>\n+    <div className=\"space-y-4 rounded border border-black/15 bg-white/30 p-5\">\n+      <label className=\"block text-sm font-medium\">Student\n+        <select className=\"mt-2 block w-full rounded border border-black/20 bg-white p-2\" value={studentId}\n+          onChange={(event) => { setStudentId(event.target.value); setFeedback(''); }} disabled={studentsLoading || busy}>\n+          <option value=\"\">Choose a student</option>\n+          {students.filter((student) => student.role !== 'admin').map((student) => <option key={student.class_id} value={student.class_id}>\n+            {student.display_name || student.email || student.class_id}{student.display_name && student.email ? ` (${student.email})` : ''}\n+          </option>)}\n+        </select>\n+      </label>\n+      {studentsError && <p role=\"alert\">Could not load students: {studentsError}</p>}\n+      {assignments.isError && <p role=\"alert\">Could not check this student’s current access. <button className=\"underline\" onClick={() => void assignments.refetch()}>Try again</button></p>}\n+      {current ? <div className=\"space-y-3\"><p className=\"text-sm\">{current.unlock_at && new Date(current.unlock_at) > new Date()\n+        ? `Assigned. Available from ${new Date(current.unlock_at).toLocaleDateString('en-CA')}.`\n+        : 'Reading Center is assigned to this student.'}</p>\n+        <button className=\"rounded border border-black/25 px-4 py-2 text-sm\" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void revoke()}>Remove access</button>\n+      </div> : <>\n+        <label className=\"block text-sm font-medium\">Start date (optional)\n+          <input type=\"date\" className=\"mt-2 block rounded border border-black/20 bg-white p-2\" value={scheduled} onChange={(event) => setScheduled(event.target.value)} disabled={busy} />\n+        </label>\n+        <button className=\"rounded bg-black px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white disabled:opacity-40\" onClick={() => void assign()}\n+          disabled={!studentId || busy || assignments.isPending || assignments.isError}>{busy ? 'Assigning…' : 'Assign Reading Center'}</button>\n+      </>}\n+      {feedback && <p role=\"status\" className=\"text-sm\">{feedback}</p>}\n+    </div>\n+  </div>;\n+}\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/App.tsx\n@@\n const TeacherWorkspaceShell = safeLazy(() => import(\"./pages/teacher/shell/TeacherWorkspaceShell\"));\n+const TeacherReadingCenter = safeLazy(() => import(\"./pages/teacher/tools/TeacherReadingCenter\"));\n+const ReadingCenterApp = safeLazy(() => import(\"./features/reading-center\"));\n+import ReadingCenterGate from '@/components/ReadingCenterGate';\n@@\n-const OVERLAY_FREE_PREFIXES = [\"/book/\", \"/classroom\", \"/drill\", \"/rc\", \"/section/\"];\n+const OVERLAY_FREE_PREFIXES = [\"/book/\", \"/classroom\", \"/drill\", \"/rc\", \"/section/\", \"/reading-center\"];\n@@\n-                  {/* Full-screen takeovers: actual LSAT question-doing + activity\n+                  <Route path=\"/reading-center/*\" element={<ReadingCenterGate><ReadingCenterApp /></ReadingCenterGate>} />\n+                  {/* Full-screen takeovers: actual LSAT question-doing + activity\n@@\n                       <Route index element={<TeacherWorkspaceIndex />} />\n+                      <Route path=\"tools\" element={<Navigate to=\"/teacher/tools/reading-center\" replace />} />\n+                      <Route path=\"tools/reading-center\" element={<TeacherReadingCenter />} />\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/pages/teacher/shell/TeacherWorkspaceShell.tsx\n@@\n   Sparkles,\n+  BookOpenText,\n@@\n   { to: '/teacher/library', label: 'Library', Icon: Library },\n+  { to: '/teacher/tools', label: 'Reading Center', Icon: BookOpenText },\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/pages/teacher/tools/TeacherReadingCenter.tsx\n@@\n-  const { students, loading: studentsLoading, error: studentsError } = useHubStudents();\n+  const { rows: students, loading: studentsLoading, error: studentsError } = useHubStudents();\n@@\n-      {studentsError && <p role=\"alert\">Could not load students: {studentsError}</p>}\n+      {studentsError && <p role=\"alert\">Could not load students: {studentsError.message}</p>}\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/supabase/migrations/20261013220000_reading_center_assignment_access.sql\n+-- Whole-tool access is issued through immutable Classroom source fields.\n+-- Re-runnable; no existing student reading data is removed.\n+begin;\n+\n+create or replace function public.has_reading_center_access()\n+returns boolean language sql stable security definer set search_path = public\n+as $$\n+  select auth.uid() is not null and (\n+    public.has_role(auth.uid(), 'admin') or exists (\n+      select 1 from public.classroom_items ci\n+      where ci.student_id = auth.uid() and ci.type = 'activity'\n+        and ci.source_table = 'reading_center' and ci.source_id = 'library'\n+        and ci.visible_to_student and ci.status in ('assigned','in_progress','completed')\n+        and (ci.unlock_at is null or ci.unlock_at <= now())\n+    )\n+  );\n+$$;\n+revoke all on function public.has_reading_center_access() from public, anon;\n+grant execute on function public.has_reading_center_access() to authenticated;\n+\n+-- This partial unique index plus an advisory transaction lock prevents double\n+-- assignments from two tabs. Revocation preserves the previous item as history.\n+create unique index if not exists reading_center_one_active_assignment\n+on public.classroom_items (student_id)\n+where source_table = 'reading_center' and source_id = 'library'\n+  and visible_to_student and status in ('assigned','in_progress','completed');\n+\n+create or replace function public.assign_reading_center(\n+  p_student_id uuid, p_unlock_at timestamptz default null,\n+  p_title text default 'Reading Center',\n+  p_description text default 'Practice reading articles with five reading modes, bookmarks and reading goals.'\n+) returns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public\n+as $$\n+declare v_item public.classroom_items%rowtype;\n+begin\n+  if auth.uid() is null or not public.has_role(auth.uid(),'admin') then\n+    raise exception 'Only an administrator may assign Reading Center' using errcode = '42501';\n+  end if;\n+  if not exists (select 1 from auth.users where id = p_student_id) then\n+    raise exception 'Student account not found' using errcode = '22023';\n+  end if;\n+  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('reading-center:' || p_student_id::text, 0));\n+  select * into v_item from public.classroom_items\n+    where student_id = p_student_id and type = 'activity'\n+      and source_table = 'reading_center' and source_id = 'library'\n+      and visible_to_student and status in ('assigned','in_progress','completed')\n+    limit 1;\n+  if found then return jsonb_build_object('item',to_jsonb(v_item),'reused',true); end if;\n+  insert into public.classroom_items (\n+    student_id,class_id,created_by,type,title,description,source_table,source_id,\n+    status,visible_to_student,unlock_at,metadata_json\n+  ) values (\n+    p_student_id,p_student_id,auth.uid(),'activity',coalesce(nullif(trim(p_title),''),'Reading Center'),\n+    p_description,'reading_center','library','assigned',true,p_unlock_at,\n+    jsonb_build_object('assigned_route','/reading-center')\n+  ) returning * into v_item;\n+  return jsonb_build_object('item',to_jsonb(v_item),'reused',false);\n+end;\n+$$;\n+revoke all on function public.assign_reading_center(uuid,timestamptz,text,text) from public,anon;\n+grant execute on function public.assign_reading_center(uuid,timestamptz,text,text) to authenticated;\n+\n+create or replace function public.revoke_reading_center(p_student_id uuid)\n+returns void language plpgsql security definer set search_path = public\n+as $$\n+begin\n+  if auth.uid() is null or not public.has_role(auth.uid(),'admin') then\n+    raise exception 'Only an administrator may remove Reading Center access' using errcode = '42501';\n+  end if;\n+  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('reading-center:' || p_student_id::text, 0));\n+  update public.classroom_items set visible_to_student=false,status='archived'\n+    where student_id=p_student_id and source_table='reading_center' and source_id='library'\n+      and (visible_to_student or status <> 'archived');\n+end;\n+$$;\n+revoke all on function public.revoke_reading_center(uuid) from public,anon;\n+grant execute on function public.revoke_reading_center(uuid) to authenticated;\n+\n+alter table public.reading_center_passages add column if not exists owner_id uuid references auth.users(id) on delete cascade;\n+alter table public.reading_center_passages add column if not exists genre text;\n+alter table public.reading_center_bookmarks add column if not exists note text;\n+\n+create table if not exists public.reading_center_tags (\n+  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\n+  name text not null check (length(trim(name)) between 1 and 100),\n+  color int not null default 0 check (color between 0 and 24),\n+  primary key (user_id,name)\n+);\n+create table if not exists public.reading_center_passage_metadata (\n+  user_id uuid not null references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\n+  passage_id uuid not null references public.reading_center_passages(id) on delete cascade,\n+  tags text[] not null default '{}', hidden boolean not null default false,\n+  title_override text, body_override text, author_override text, genre_override text, cover_url_override text,\n+  primary key (user_id,passage_id)\n+);\n+create table if not exists public.reading_center_profile (\n+  user_id uuid primary key references auth.users(id) on delete cascade,\n+  display_name text not null default ''\n+);\n+\n+-- Remove v1's broad signed-in read policy. Owners need an active grant too.\n+drop policy if exists \"rc passages read published\" on public.reading_center_passages;\n+drop policy if exists \"rc passages assigned read\" on public.reading_center_passages;\n+create policy \"rc passages assigned read\" on public.reading_center_passages for select to authenticated\n+using (public.has_role(auth.uid(),'admin') or (public.has_reading_center_access() and\n+  (owner_id=auth.uid() or (owner_id is null and is_published))));\n+drop policy if exists \"rc passages owner insert\" on public.reading_center_passages;\n+create policy \"rc passages owner insert\" on public.reading_center_passages for insert to authenticated\n+with check (owner_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access());\n+drop policy if exists \"rc passages owner update\" on public.reading_center_passages;\n+create policy \"rc passages owner update\" on public.reading_center_passages for update to authenticated\n+using (owner_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access())\n+with check (owner_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access());\n+drop policy if exists \"rc passages owner delete\" on public.reading_center_passages;\n+create policy \"rc passages owner delete\" on public.reading_center_passages for delete to authenticated\n+using (owner_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access());\n+\n+-- Ensure unassigned users cannot write their own progress via direct API.\n+do $$\n+declare t text; n text;\n+begin\n+  foreach t in array array['preferences','progress','bookmarks','sessions','tags','passage_metadata','profile'] loop\n+    execute format('alter table public.reading_center_%I enable row level security', t);\n+    n := case t when 'preferences' then 'prefs' when 'passage_metadata' then 'metadata' else t end;\n+    execute format('drop policy if exists %I on public.reading_center_%I', 'rc ' || n || ' own', t);\n+    execute format('create policy %I on public.reading_center_%I for all to authenticated using (user_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access()) with check (user_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access())', 'rc ' || n || ' own',t);\n+    execute format('drop policy if exists %I on public.reading_center_%I','rc ' || n || ' admin read',t);\n+    execute format('create policy %I on public.reading_center_%I for select to authenticated using (public.has_role(auth.uid(),''admin''))','rc ' || n || ' admin read',t);\n+    execute format('grant select,insert,update,delete on public.reading_center_%I to authenticated',t);\n+  end loop;\n+end;\n+$$;\n+grant select,insert,update,delete on public.reading_center_passages to authenticated;\n+\n+-- Per-user metadata must reference a passage that this user may actually read.\n+drop policy if exists \"rc metadata own\" on public.reading_center_passage_metadata;\n+create policy \"rc metadata own\" on public.reading_center_passage_metadata for all to authenticated\n+using (user_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access())\n+with check (user_id=auth.uid() and public.has_reading_center_access() and exists (\n+  select 1 from public.reading_center_passages p where p.id=passage_id\n+));\n+\n+create or replace function public.reading_center_rename_tag(p_old_name text,p_new_name text,p_color int)\n+returns void language plpgsql security invoker set search_path=public\n+as $$\n+begin\n+  if not public.has_reading_center_access() then raise exception 'Reading Center is not assigned' using errcode='42501'; end if;\n+  if not exists(select 1 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name) then\n+    raise exception 'Tag not found' using errcode='22023';\n+  end if;\n+  insert into public.reading_center_tags(user_id,name,color) values(auth.uid(),trim(p_new_name),p_color)\n+    on conflict(user_id,name) do update set color=excluded.color;\n+  update public.reading_center_passage_metadata set tags=array(\n+    select distinct x from unnest(array_replace(tags,p_old_name,trim(p_new_name))) as x\n+  ) where user_id=auth.uid() and p_old_name=any(tags);\n+  if p_old_name<>trim(p_new_name) then\n+    delete from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name;\n+  end if;\n+end;\n+$$;\n+create or replace function public.reading_center_delete_tag(p_name text)\n+returns void language plpgsql security invoker set search_path=public\n+as $$\n+begin\n+  if not public.has_reading_center_access() then raise exception 'Reading Center is not assigned' using errcode='42501'; end if;\n+  update public.reading_center_passage_metadata set tags=array_remove(tags,p_name)\n+    where user_id=auth.uid() and p_name=any(tags);\n+  delete from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_name;\n+end;\n+$$;\n+revoke all on function public.reading_center_rename_tag(text,text,int), public.reading_center_delete_tag(text) from public,anon;\n+grant execute on function public.reading_center_rename_tag(text,text,int), public.reading_center_delete_tag(text) to authenticated;\n+\n+commit;\n*** End Patch"));

({ supabase: {\n+  rpc: state.rpc,\n+  from: () => { const q = { select: () => q, eq: () => q,\n+    single: async () => ({ data: state.saved, error: state.readError }) }; return q; },\n+} }));\n+import { assignReadingCenter, hasReadingCenterAccess, isReadingCenterAssignment, revokeReadingCenter } from './readingCenterAccess';\n+\n+const item = { id: 'item-1', student_id: 'student-1', type: 'activity', source_table: 'reading_center',\n+  source_id: 'library', visible_to_student: true, status: 'assigned' };\n+beforeEach(() => { state.rpc.mockReset(); state.saved = { ...item }; state.readError = null; });\n+describe('Reading Center assignment contract', () => {\n+  it('verifies the actual student launcher after the grant RPC', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: { item, reused: false }, error: null });\n+    expect(await assignReadingCenter({ studentId: 'student-1' })).toEqual({ item, reused: false });\n+    expect(state.rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith('assign_reading_center', expect.objectContaining({ p_student_id: 'student-1', p_unlock_at: null }));\n+  });\n+  it('returns the existing verified item for an idempotent retry', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: { item, reused: true }, error: null });\n+    expect((await assignReadingCenter({ studentId: 'student-1' })).reused).toBe(true);\n+  });\n+  it('does not report success for a different student or a hidden launcher', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: { item: { ...item, student_id: 'other' } }, error: null });\n+    await expect(assignReadingCenter({ studentId: 'student-1' })).rejects.toThrow('could not be verified');\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: { item }, error: null });\n+    state.saved = { ...item, visible_to_student: false };\n+    await expect(assignReadingCenter({ studentId: 'student-1' })).rejects.toThrow('not available');\n+  });\n+  it('does not claim verification when the database readback fails', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: { item }, error: null }); state.readError = new Error('database unavailable');\n+    await expect(assignReadingCenter({ studentId: 'student-1' })).rejects.toThrow('database unavailable');\n+  });\n+  it('sends the chosen local start date as the access unlock time', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: { item }, error: null });\n+    await assignReadingCenter({ studentId: 'student-1', scheduledForLocalDate: '2026-10-14' });\n+    expect(state.rpc.mock.calls[0][1].p_unlock_at).toEqual(new Date(2026, 9, 14).toISOString());\n+  });\n+  it('uses the database decision and reports access failures', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: false, error: null }); expect(await hasReadingCenterAccess()).toBe(false);\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: true, error: null }); expect(await hasReadingCenterAccess()).toBe(true);\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: new Error('permission denied') });\n+    await expect(hasReadingCenterAccess()).rejects.toThrow('permission denied');\n+  });\n+  it('revokes through the admin-only RPC', async () => {\n+    state.rpc.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: null }); await revokeReadingCenter('student-1');\n+    expect(state.rpc).toHaveBeenCalledWith('revoke_reading_center', { p_student_id: 'student-1' });\n+  });\n+  it('requires immutable source fields rather than editable metadata', () => {\n+    expect(isReadingCenterAssignment(item as never)).toBe(true);\n+    expect(isReadingCenterAssignment({ ...item, source_table: 'activity_assignments' } as never)).toBe(false);\n+    expect(isReadingCenterAssignment({ ...item, source_id: 'different-tool' } as never)).toBe(false);\n+  });\n+});\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/components/ReadingCenterGate.test.tsx\n+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n+import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';\n+import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';\n+import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router-dom';\n+const state = vi.hoisted(() => ({ user: 'student-1', admin: false, allowed: false, fail: false, rpc: vi.fn() }));\n+vi.mock('@/contexts/AuthContext', () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ user: { id: state.user }, authReady: true }) }));\n+vi.mock('@/hooks/useUserPermissions', () => ({ useUserPermissions: () => ({ is_admin: state.admin, loading: false }) }));\n+vi.mock('@/components/ProtectedRoute', () => ({ default: ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) => <>{children}</> }));\n+vi.mock('@/components/LockedModule', () => ({ default: () => <div>Reading Center locked</div> }));\n+vi.mock('@/integrations/supabase/client', () => ({ supabase: { rpc: state.rpc } }));\n+import ReadingCenterGate from './ReadingCenterGate';\n+function open(cache = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })) {\n+  const view = render(<QueryClientProvider client={cache}><MemoryRouter initialEntries={['/reading-center/read/example']}>\n+    <ReadingCenterGate><div>Reading tool opened</div></ReadingCenterGate>\n+  </MemoryRouter></QueryClientProvider>);\n+  return { ...view, cache };\n+}\n+beforeEach(() => { state.user = 'student-1'; state.admin = false; state.allowed = false; state.fail = false;\n+  state.rpc.mockReset().mockImplementation(async () => ({ data: state.allowed, error: state.fail ? new Error('offline') : null })); });\n+describe('assignment-only Reading Center route', () => {\n+  it('denies direct reader links to unassigned students', async () => { open(); expect(await screen.findByText('Reading Center locked')).toBeTruthy(); });\n+  it('opens an assigned reader on a fresh load', async () => { state.allowed = true; open(); expect(await screen.findByText('Reading tool opened')).toBeTruthy(); });\n+  it('does not reuse another student’s cached access', async () => {\n+    const view = open(); expect(await screen.findByText('Reading Center locked')).toBeTruthy(); view.unmount();\n+    view.cache.setQueryData(['reading-center-access', 'student-1'], true); state.user = 'student-2'; open(view.cache);\n+    expect(await screen.findByText('Reading Center locked')).toBeTruthy();\n+  });\n+  it('closes the mounted tool when access is revoked and rechecked', async () => {\n+    state.allowed = true; const view = open(); expect(await screen.findByText('Reading tool opened')).toBeTruthy();\n+    state.allowed = false; await view.cache.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['reading-center-access'] });\n+    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText('Reading tool opened')).toBeNull());\n+    expect(screen.getByText('Reading Center locked')).toBeTruthy();\n+  });\n+  it('fails closed and offers a retry when the grant cannot be checked', async () => { state.fail = true; open(); expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toBeTruthy(); expect(screen.queryByText('Reading tool opened')).toBeNull(); });\n+  it('allows the teacher without issuing any student assignment', () => { state.admin = true; open(); expect(screen.getByText('Reading tool opened')).toBeTruthy(); expect(state.rpc).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); });\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/src/lib/assignWork.readingCenter.test.ts\n+import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';\n+const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({ assign: vi.fn(), notify: vi.fn() }));\n+vi.mock('@/lib/readingCenterAccess', () => ({ assignReadingCenter: mocks.assign, READING_CENTER_PATH: '/reading-center' }));\n+vi.mock('@/lib/classroomNotify', () => ({ notifyStudentOfClassroomItem: mocks.notify }));\n+import { assignWork } from './assignWork';\n+const input = { teacherId: 'teacher', studentIds: ['student'], item: { kind: 'reading_center' as const } };\n+beforeEach(() => {\n+  mocks.assign.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ item: { id: 'launcher', title: 'Reading Center', description: 'Reading practice' }, reused: false });\n+  mocks.notify.mockReset().mockResolvedValue({ inbox_ok: true, bell_ok: true, email_ok: true });\n+});\n+describe('assignWork Reading Center', () => {\n+  it('returns the verified Classroom launcher and notifies only once', async () => {\n+    const first = await assignWork(input);\n+    expect(first.ok[0]).toMatchObject({ studentId: 'student', classroomItemId: 'launcher', route: '/reading-center' });\n+    mocks.assign.mockResolvedValue({ item: { id: 'launcher', title: 'Reading Center' }, reused: true });\n+    const retry = await assignWork(input);\n+    expect(retry.ok[0].classroomItemId).toBe('launcher'); expect(mocks.notify).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n+  });\n+  it('keeps a verified grant when notification delivery fails', async () => {\n+    mocks.notify.mockRejectedValue(new Error('notification unavailable'));\n+    const result = await assignWork(input);\n+    expect(result.failed).toEqual([]); expect(result.ok[0].notify).toMatchObject({ inbox_ok: false, bell_ok: false, email_ok: false });\n+  });\n+  it('does not notify or report success if the actual assignment cannot be verified', async () => {\n+    mocks.assign.mockRejectedValue(new Error('readback failed'));\n+    const result = await assignWork(input);\n+    expect(result.ok).toEqual([]); expect(result.failed[0].error).toBe('readback failed'); expect(mocks.notify).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n+  });\n+  it('deduplicates selected students and supports explicit silent assignments', async () => {\n+    const result = await assignWork({ ...input, studentIds: ['student', 'student'], notify: false });\n+    expect(result.ok).toHaveLength(1); expect(mocks.assign).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); expect(mocks.notify).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

## assistant

Implemented assignment-only integration outside the feature folder.

- `/reading-center/*` requires a verified assignment or admin access.
- Teacher Reading Center page supports student selection, scheduled assignment, preview, and access removal.
- Classroom cards open the correct tool.
- Assignment RPC is atomic, verified by readback, and prevents duplicate grants/notifications.
- RLS blocks unassigned reads/writes and protects private student passages, progress, tags, bookmarks, and profile.
- Revocation preserves saved work.

Changed migrations: `20261013120000_reading_center_v1.sql` is rerunnable; `20261013220000_reading_center_assignment_access.sql` adds secure access and port schema.

**76 focused tests passed**, including existing assignment/resolver tests. Browser verification, completed feature typecheck, and live migration/security probes remain with the parent. No commits, deployments, notifications, or real student assignments performed.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"{\\n  \\"rows\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\"definition\\": \\"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_activity_mirror_access()\\\\n RETURNS trigger\\\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\\\n SECURITY DEFINER\\\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\\\nAS $function$\\\\ndeclare v_revoked timestamptz; v_student uuid; v_activity boolean;\\\\nbegin\\\\n  v_activity := NEW.source_table = \'activity_assignments\' or NEW.type = \'activity\';\\\\n  -- Existing orphan mirrors are historical data. Unrelated edits do not grant\\\\n  -- access; newly visible or rebound mirrors must satisfy the source gate.\\\\n  if TG_OP = \'UPDATE\' then\\\\n    v_activity := coalesce(v_activity,false) or OLD.source_table = \'activity_assignments\' or OLD.type = \'activity\';\\\\n    if NEW.visible_to_student is not distinct from OLD.visible_to_student\\\\n       and NEW.source_table is not distinct from OLD.source_table\\\\n       and NEW.source_id is not distinct from OLD.source_id\\\\n       and NEW.type is not distinct from OLD.type\\\\n       and NEW.student_id is not distinct from OLD.student_id then\\\\n      return NEW;\\\\n    end if;\\\\n  end if;\\\\n  if NEW.visible_to_student and v_activity then\\\\n    if NEW.source_id is null or\\\\n       (NEW.source_table is distinct from \'activity_assignments\' and NEW.type is distinct from \'activity\') then\\\\n      raise exception \'Visible Activity mirror source cannot be cleared\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\\\n    end if;\\\\n    select revoked_at, student_id into v_revoked, v_student\\\\n      from public.activity_assignments where id::text = NEW.source_id for share;\\\\n    if not found or v_revoked is not null or v_student is distinct from NEW.student_id then\\\\n      raise exception \'Activity mirror must name an available source for this student\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\\\n    end if;\\\\n  end if;\\\\n  return NEW;\\\\nend;\\\\n$function$\\\\n\\"\\n    }\\n  ]\\n}"}],"structuredContent":{"rows":[{"definition":"CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_activity_mirror_access()\\n RETURNS trigger\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\n SECURITY DEFINER\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\nAS $function$\\ndeclare v_revoked timestamptz; v_student uuid; v_activity boolean;\\nbegin\\n  v_activity := NEW.source_table = \'activity_assignments\' or NEW.type = \'activity\';\\n  -- Existing orphan mirrors are historical data. Unrelated edits do not grant\\n  -- access; newly visible or rebound mirrors must satisfy the source gate.\\n  if TG_OP = \'UPDATE\' then\\n    v_activity := coalesce(v_activity,false) or OLD.source_table = \'activity_assignments\' or OLD.type = \'activity\';\\n    if NEW.visible_to_student is not distinct from OLD.visible_to_student\\n       and NEW.source_table is not distinct from OLD.source_table\\n       and NEW.source_id is not distinct from OLD.source_id\\n       and NEW.type is not distinct from OLD.type\\n       and NEW.student_id is not distinct from OLD.student_id then\\n      return NEW;\\n    end if;\\n  end if;\\n  if NEW.visible_to_student and v_activity then\\n    if NEW.source_id is null or\\n       (NEW.source_table is distinct from \'activity_assignments\' and NEW.type is distinct from \'activity\') then\\n      raise exception \'Visible Activity mirror source cannot be cleared\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\n    end if;\\n    select revoked_at, student_id into v_revoked, v_student\\n      from public.activity_assignments where id::text = NEW.source_id for share;\\n    if not found or v_revoked is not null or v_student is distinct from NEW.student_id then\\n      raise exception \'Activity mirror must name an available source for this student\' using errcode = \'42501\';\\n    end if;\\n  end if;\\n  return NEW;\\nend;\\n$function$\\n"}]},"isError":false}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/supabase/migrations/20261013222000_reading_center_activity_mirror_guard.sql\n+-- Reading Center grants are Classroom activities but have no Activity Centre\n+-- assignment row. Admit their exact immutable source without weakening the\n+-- existing Activity Centre mirror checks.\n+begin;\n+create or replace function public.guard_activity_mirror_access()\n+returns trigger language plpgsql security definer set search_path=public\n+as $function$\n+declare v_revoked timestamptz; v_student uuid; v_activity boolean;\n+begin\n+  if NEW.type='activity' and NEW.source_table='reading_center' and NEW.source_id='library' then\n+    if auth.uid() is not null and public.has_role(auth.uid(),'admin') then\n+      return NEW;\n+    end if;\n+    -- Student progress may advance an existing grant. Other triggers enforce\n+    -- the allowed statuses; this exception never creates or rebinds access.\n+    if TG_OP='UPDATE' and OLD.type='activity' and OLD.source_table='reading_center' and OLD.source_id='library'\n+      and NEW.student_id is not distinct from OLD.student_id\n+      and NEW.created_by is not distinct from OLD.created_by\n+      and NEW.visible_to_student is not distinct from OLD.visible_to_student\n+      and NEW.unlock_at is not distinct from OLD.unlock_at then\n+      return NEW;\n+    end if;\n+  end if;\n+  v_activity := NEW.source_table = 'activity_assignments' or NEW.type = 'activity';\n+  -- Existing orphan mirrors are historical data. Unrelated edits do not grant\n+  -- access; newly visible or rebound mirrors must satisfy the source gate.\n+  if TG_OP = 'UPDATE' then\n+    v_activity := coalesce(v_activity,false) or OLD.source_table = 'activity_assignments' or OLD.type = 'activity';\n+    if NEW.visible_to_student is not distinct from OLD.visible_to_student\n+       and NEW.source_table is not distinct from OLD.source_table\n+       and NEW.source_id is not distinct from OLD.source_id\n+       and NEW.type is not distinct from OLD.type\n+       and NEW.student_id is not distinct from OLD.student_id then\n+      return NEW;\n+    end if;\n+  end if;\n+  if NEW.visible_to_student and v_activity then\n+    if NEW.source_id is null or\n+       (NEW.source_table is distinct from 'activity_assignments' and NEW.type is distinct from 'activity') then\n+      raise exception 'Visible Activity mirror source cannot be cleared' using errcode = '42501';\n+    end if;\n+    select revoked_at, student_id into v_revoked, v_student\n+      from public.activity_assignments where id::text = NEW.source_id for share;\n+    if not found or v_revoked is not null or v_student is distinct from NEW.student_id then\n+      raise exception 'Activity mirror must name an available source for this student' using errcode = '42501';\n+    end if;\n+  end if;\n+  return NEW;\n+end;\n+$function$;\n+commit;\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/docs/agent/reading-center-20261013/security-receipt.md\n+# Reading Center security acceptance, 2026-10-13\n+\n+Verified against Lovable project `53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76`, live backend `atrcqxpmqrynmtvvyjju`, through its free SQL query tool.\n+\n+The first rollback-only probe found a real integration defect: the deployed `guard_activity_mirror_access()` rejected Reading Center Classroom items because it required an Activity Centre assignment row for every item of type `activity`. The exact error was SQLSTATE `42501`, “Activity mirror must name an available source for this student.” The failed transaction left zero Reading Center assignments and zero private test passages.\n+\n+Migration `20261013222000_reading_center_activity_mirror_guard.sql` preserves the existing guard and adds an exception for the exact immutable `activity` / `reading_center` / `library` source. Only administrators create that grant. Student updates may advance progress on the existing grant while preserving the student, creator, visibility and unlock time; the Reading Center update guard separately protects status and immutable fields.\n+\n+After the repair was applied, `security-probes.sql` executed successfully and returned:\n+\n+```json\n+{\n+  \"result\": \"30 live SQL assertions passed; all test writes rolled back\",\n+  \"remaining_assignments\": 0,\n+  \"remaining_private_fixtures\": 0\n+}\n+```\n+\n+Assertions cover anonymous API/RPC denial, unassigned reader/writer denial, administrator-only assignment, repeat assignment reusing one launcher, assigned access and saved own preferences, foreign preference rejection, private imported passage isolation, wrong-student launcher denial, revocation, refusal to restore archived access, scheduled unlock denial, refusal to change the unlock time, and refusal to derive entitlement from editable metadata or forged source fields.\n+\n+The probe used configured admin/tester identities with local transaction claims, never credentials in SQL. Every fixture, access grant and data write was inside `BEGIN` / `ROLLBACK`. No notifications were invoked. This receipt verifies database access and assignment behavior; signed-in browser acceptance is separate.\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-reading-center/supabase/migrations/20261013223000_reading_center_tag_rename_validation.sql\n+-- Match the reader's tag editor: changing a color keeps the tag name;\n+-- renaming cannot merge or overwrite an existing tag.\n+begin;\n+create or replace function public.reading_center_rename_tag(p_old_name text,p_new_name text,p_color int)\n+returns void language plpgsql security invoker set search_path=public\n+as $$\n+declare v_name text := trim(p_new_name);\n+begin\n+  if not public.has_reading_center_access() then\n+    raise exception 'Reading Center is not assigned' using errcode='42501';\n+  end if;\n+  if v_name is null or length(v_name) not between 1 and 100 then\n+    raise exception 'Choose a tag name between 1 and 100 characters' using errcode='22023';\n+  end if;\n+  if p_color is null or p_color not between 0 and 24 then\n+    raise exception 'Choose a valid tag color' using errcode='22023';\n+  end if;\n+  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('reading-center-tags:' || auth.uid()::text,0));\n+  if not exists(select 1 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name) then\n+    raise exception 'Tag not found' using errcode='22023';\n+  end if;\n+  if v_name=p_old_name then\n+    update public.reading_center_tags set color=p_color where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name;\n+    return;\n+  end if;\n+  if exists(select 1 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=v_name) then\n+    raise exception 'A tag with that name already exists' using errcode='23505';\n+  end if;\n+  -- A direct concurrent tag creation is also protected by the unique key.\n+  -- No conflict overwrite is allowed, so a collision rolls back the rename.\n+  insert into public.reading_center_tags(user_id,name,color) values(auth.uid(),v_name,p_color);\n+  update public.reading_center_passage_metadata set tags=array(\n+    select distinct x from unnest(array_replace(tags,p_old_name,v_name)) as x\n+  ) where user_id=auth.uid() and p_old_name=any(tags);\n+  delete from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name=p_old_name;\n+end;\n+$$;\n+revoke all on function public.reading_center_rename_tag(text,text,int) from public,anon;\n+grant execute on function public.reading_center_rename_tag(text,text,int) to authenticated;\n+commit;\n*** End Patch"));

All tag/metadata writes roll back.\n+begin;\n+create temp table rc_tag_results(name text,passed boolean);\n+grant select,insert on rc_tag_results to authenticated;\n+create or replace function pg_temp.rc_tag_assert(p_name text,p_passed boolean)\n+returns void language plpgsql as $$ begin\n+  if p_passed is distinct from true then raise exception 'RC tag assertion failed: %',p_name; end if;\n+  insert into rc_tag_results values(p_name,true);\n+end; $$;\n+create or replace function pg_temp.rc_tag_error(p_sql text,p_code text)\n+returns boolean language plpgsql as $$ declare actual text; begin\n+  execute p_sql; return false;\n+exception when others then\n+  get stacked diagnostics actual=returned_sqlstate;\n+  return actual=p_code;\n+end; $$;\n+set local role authenticated;\n+select set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub','d763bfe6-d710-4c4b-90ff-4b3a20929211',true);\n+select set_config('request.jwt.claims','{\"sub\":\"d763bfe6-d710-4c4b-90ff-4b3a20929211\",\"role\":\"authenticated\"}',true);\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('tester has current assigned access',public.has_reading_center_access());\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('temporary fixture names absent before probe',(\n+  select count(*)=0 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid()\n+    and name in ('RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Target 20261013','RC Probe Renamed 20261013')\n+));\n+insert into public.reading_center_tags(user_id,name,color) values\n+  (auth.uid(),'RC Probe Original 20261013',1),(auth.uid(),'RC Probe Target 20261013',2);\n+insert into public.reading_center_passage_metadata(user_id,passage_id,tags)\n+select auth.uid(),id,array['RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Target 20261013']\n+from public.reading_center_passages where slug='living-in-a-rational-society'\n+on conflict(user_id,passage_id) do update set tags=excluded.tags;\n+\n+select public.reading_center_rename_tag('RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Original 20261013',24);\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('same-name maximum color updates without renaming',(\n+  select color=24 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name='RC Probe Original 20261013'\n+));\n+select public.reading_center_rename_tag('RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Original 20261013',0);\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('same-name minimum color updates without renaming',(\n+  select color=0 from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name='RC Probe Original 20261013'\n+));\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('duplicate target rejects with unique violation',pg_temp.rc_tag_error(\n+  $q$select public.reading_center_rename_tag('RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Target 20261013',5)$q$,'23505'));\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('duplicate rejection preserves both tag names and colors',(\n+  select count(*)=2 and bool_and(case name when 'RC Probe Original 20261013' then color=0 else color=2 end)\n+  from public.reading_center_tags where user_id=auth.uid() and name in ('RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Target 20261013')\n+));\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('duplicate rejection preserves passage tag array',(\n+  select m.tags=array['RC Probe Original 20261013','RC Probe Target 20261013']\n+  from public.reading_center_passage_metadata m join public.reading_center_passages p on p.id=m.passage_id\n+  where m.user_id=auth.uid() and p.slug='living-in-a-rational-society'\n+));\n+\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('unknown source tag rejected',pg_temp.rc_tag_error(\n+  $q$select public.reading_center_rename_tag('RC Probe Missing 20261013','New name',5)$q$,'22023'));\n+select pg_temp.rc_tag_assert('blank name rejected',pg_temp.rc_tag_error(\n+  $q$select public.reading_center_rename_tag('RC Probe Original 20261013',' [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 11.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"62db12","wall_time_seconds":0.391041,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":613,"output":"/**\\r\\n * The Classroom Home model.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The page reads five things and states them once each: what to pick up, what\\r\\n * is assigned, what the coach said, when the next session is, and what the week\\r\\n * holds. Everything here is derived from work that actually exists in the\\r\\n * student\'s own record. Nothing on this page may be invented: a count, a due\\r\\n * date, or a progress bar that cannot be traced to a row is omitted, not\\r\\n * guessed. The view has honest empty variants for every block.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { localDayISO, parseLocalDay } from \\"@/lib/localDate\\";\\r\\nimport type { SectionBriefing } from \\"@/lib/sectionBriefing\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type HomeKind =\\r\\n  | \\"activity\\"\\r\\n  | \\"guided\\"\\r\\n  | \\"homework\\"\\r\\n  | \\"ta\\"\\r\\n  | \\"bootcamp\\"\\r\\n  | \\"plan\\"\\r\\n  | \\"recap\\"\\r\\n  | \\"note\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Which of the three status dots a row carries. */\\r\\nexport type HomeTone = \\"activity\\" | \\"drill\\" | \\"recap\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeRow {\\r\\n  key: string;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  kind: HomeKind;\\r\\n  tone: HomeTone;\\r\\n  /** \\"Activity · twelve prompts · due today\\" */\\r\\n  meta: string;\\r\\n  route: string;\\r\\n  /** \\"Open\\" for work, \\"Read\\" for a recap or note. */\\r\\n  actionLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** Orange pill (the one thing to do next) versus the muted ring. */\\r\\n  emphasis: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Never opened. Drives the \\"N NEW\\" badge; derived, never stored twice. */\\r\\n  isNew: boolean;\\r\\n  dueToday: boolean;\\r\\n  overdue: boolean;\\r\\n  /** Saved work evidence, not a click or page view. */\\r\\n  workStatus?: \'assigned\' | \'in_progress\';\\r\\n  /** Existing feed deadline, retained for the Continue action. */\\r\\n  dueAt?: string | null;\\r\\n  /** Work the student can close out by hand; reading matter cannot. */\\r\\n  canComplete?: boolean;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * A timed section\'s instructions, carried on the row so Home can raise them\\r\\n   * over the stage rather than sending the student to a page to read them.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  briefing?: SectionBriefing;\\r\\n\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeProgress {\\r\\n  /** Present only when both halves are provable. */\\r\\n  completed: number | null;\\r\\n  total: number;\\r\\n  /** \\"prompts\\", \\"questions\\" — plural noun for the count. */\\r\\n  unit: string;\\r\\n  estimateMinutes: number | null;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface HomeHero {\\r\\n  row: HomeRow;\\r\\n  eyebrow: string;\\r\\n  /** \\"ACTIVITY\\", \\"GUIDED DRILL\\" — the mono tag beside the meta line. */\\r\\n  kindLabel: string;\\r\\n  /** The row\'s meta with the kind dropped: the tag beside it already says it. */\\r\\n  meta: string;\\r\\n  prose: string | null;\\r\\n  primaryLabel: string;\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 12.7 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"813973","wall_time_seconds":0.5334925,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":531,"output":"import { useOrientation, OrientationCardView } from \\"@/components/classroom/OrientationCard\\";\\r\\nimport AnnouncementBanner from \\"@/components/classroom/AnnouncementBanner\\";\\r\\nimport ClassroomHome from \\"@/components/classroom/home/ClassroomHome\\";\\r\\nimport LockedModule from \\"@/components/LockedModule\\";\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The classroom home: the student dashboard.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * One screen that answers, in order, what to do now, what is assigned, whether\\r\\n * the coach said anything, when the next session is, and what the week holds.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Except before setup is finished. Then the intake owns the whole stage: a\\r\\n * student answering questions about their week should not be reading a week\\r\\n * they have not built yet, and the old split showed both at once. They begin\\r\\n * from the invitation, walk the questions one at a time, and the classroom\\r\\n * appears behind them when they are done.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * And except when access has been revoked. The /classroom route admits any\\r\\n * signed-in student so a locked account still has somewhere to land, but the\\r\\n * dashboard itself lists assigned work, notes and recaps, which is exactly\\r\\n * what has_classroom_access buys. Without the flag the stage shows the same\\r\\n * locked notice every other door shows.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function Classroom() {\\r\\n  const { loaded, completed, setCompleted, name, answers, save } = useOrientation();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Nothing until the profile read lands: drawing the home first and swapping\\r\\n  // it for the intake a beat later is worse than a moment of quiet.\\r\\n  if (!loaded) return null;\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!completed) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <OrientationCardView\\r\\n        initialName={name}\\r\\n        initialAnswers={answers}\\r\\n        onSave={save}\\r\\n        onDone={() => setCompleted(true)}\\r\\n        startCollapsed\\r\\n      />\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!permissions.loading && !permissions.is_admin && !permissions.has_classroom_access) {\\r\\n    return <LockedModule />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <>\\r\\n      <AnnouncementBanner />\\r\\n      <ClassroomHome />\\r\\n    </>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"72c8ea","wall_time_seconds":2.6197773,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

## assistant

Fixed the no-flags access blocker. Assigned students now get:

- A persistent Reading Center sidebar link.
- A limited Home launcher even with general Classroom access off.
- Access through the same verified assignment RPC.

Unassigned, scheduled, revoked, signed-out, loading, and failed-verification states hide the launcher. Other coursework remains gated.

**31 focused tests and final TypeScript check passed.** Source is stable for parent browser verification and publication.

## assistant

Reading Center already enters the assigned-work feed correctly. Three UI layers block My Work for accounts without general Classroom access: route guards, destination tabs, and sidebar lock state.

Minimal correction:

- Place the assigned-only link immediately after My Work; remove its Quick access placement.
- Hide that student launcher for admins; keep admin preview in Teacher Workspace.
- Let a verified Reading Center assignment open My Work’s To do/Completed tabs.
- With Classroom access off, show only the assigned Reading Center row and hide weekly plans/other coursework. Notes remain gated.
- Filter Reading Center rows through the live assignment query even when broader flags are enabled.
- Keep My Work visible while Reading Center is assigned, including customized sidebar layouts.

Files: `ClassroomLayout.tsx`, `AssignedReadingCenterLink.tsx`, `MyWorkPage.tsx`, `App.tsx`, `StudentDestination.tsx`, `destinationAccess.ts`, plus a small assignment-aware My Work gate and focused tests.

No edits made. No schema change needed.

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 21.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"d3e5f3","wall_time_seconds":0.7457825,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":851,"output":"import { Lock } from \\"lucide-react\\";\\r\\nimport { useContext } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { useLocation, useNavigate } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { Button } from \\"@/components/ui/button\\";\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\\";\\r\\nimport { DestinationHeadingContext } from \\"@/contexts/DestinationHeadingContext\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * What a student sees when a route\'s feature flag is false.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * It renders as ordinary stage content, not a full-viewport takeover: both\\r\\n * shells mount this inside their <Outlet/>, so `min-h-screen` plus a hard zinc\\r\\n * background painted a slab over the frame it is meant to sit inside. Tokens\\r\\n * only, so it reads in light and dark — the old `text-zinc-300` landed on the\\r\\n * outline variant\'s light `bg-background` and came out at 1.44:1.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * The old copy (\\"Premium Content Locked ... contact administration\\") named no\\r\\n * one and led nowhere. A locked door has to say who opens it.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A missing feature flag does not establish that the account was paused.\\r\\n * Keep the explanation neutral and offer only reachable next steps.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function LockedModule({ staffOnly = false }: { staffOnly?: boolean }) {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { pathname } = useLocation();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const canReachClassroom = permissions.has_classroom_access || permissions.is_admin;\\r\\n  const canMessage = permissions.has_chat_access || permissions.is_admin;\\r\\n  const canReachAttempts = pathname.startsWith(\\"/practice/exams\\") && pathname !== \\"/practice/exams/attempts\\";\\r\\n  const withinDestination = useContext(DestinationHeadingContext);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"flex min-h-[60vh] items-center justify-center px-6 py-16\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"max-w-sm text-center\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mx-auto mb-6 flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted\\">\\r\\n          <Lock className=\\"h-6 w-6 text-muted-foreground\\" aria-hidden=\\"true\\" />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        {withinDestination\\r\\n          ? <h2 className=\\"mb-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground\\">Not open yet</h2>\\r\\n          : <h1 className=\\"mb-2 text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground\\">Not open yet</h1>}\\r\\n        <p className=\\"text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n          {staffOnly\\r\\n            ? \\"This section is available to Joshua and administrators only.\\"\\r\\n            : `This section isn\'t available to your account.${canMessage ? \\" If that looks wrong, message Joshua.\\" : \\"\\"}`}\\r\\n        </p>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mt-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3\\">\\r\\n          {canReachAttempts ? (\\r\\n            <Button onClick={() => navigate(\\"/practice/exams/attempts\\")}>\\r\\n              View exam attempts\\r\\n            </Button>\\r\\n          ) : null}\\r\\n          {canReachClassroom ? (\\r\\n            <Button onClick={() => navigate(\\"/classroom\\")}>\\r\\n              Back to your classroom\\r\\n            </Button>\\r\\n          ) : null}\\r\\n          {/* Only offered when the student can actually reach Messages.\\r\\n              Otherwise the one next step we hand them is another locked door. */}\\r\\n          {canMessage ? (\\r\\n            <Button variant={canReachClassroom ? \\"outline\\" : \\"default\\"} onClick={() => navigate(\\"/inbox\\")}>\\r\\n              Message Joshua\\r\\n            </Button>\\r\\n          ) : null}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{}'}]

'{"i":1,"result":{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"60eb5d","wall_time_seconds":1.4276239,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1006,"output":"    // taking effect. Cached data keeps `loading` false, so neither flashes.\\r\\n    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\\r\\n    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,\\r\\n    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,\\r\\n    // Retry transient failures a couple times, but never retry a dead session\\r\\n    // (that path signs the user out).\\r\\n    retry: (count, err) =>\\r\\n      !(err instanceof SessionExpiredError) && !(err instanceof AccountArchivedError) && count < 2,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const userId = user!.id;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Pre-check: is the session still alive on the client?\\r\\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\\r\\n      if (!session) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Fetch access flags from profile\\r\\n      const { data: profile, error: profileErr } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\\"profiles\\")\\r\\n        .select([...ACCESS_FLAGS, \\"archived_at\\"].join(\\", \\"))\\r\\n        .eq(\\"class_id\\", userId)\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n      if (isSessionDeadError(profileErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\r\\n      // A non-session read failure (transient network / 5xx) must NOT be\\r\\n      // silently converted to all-false permissions; throw so TanStack Query\\r\\n      // records the error and retries instead of caching bogus denied flags.\\r\\n      if (profileErr) throw profileErr;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Fetch admin role\\r\\n      const { data: roleData, error: roleErr } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\\"user_roles\\")\\r\\n        .select(\\"role\\")\\r\\n        .eq(\\"user_id\\", userId)\\r\\n        .eq(\\"role\\", \\"admin\\")\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n      if (isSessionDeadError(roleErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\r\\n      if (roleErr) throw roleErr;\\r\\n\\r\\n      const profileRow = (profile ?? null) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n      // Archived: no flags, no app. Sign them out on the spot.\\r\\n      if (profileRow?.archived_at) throw new AccountArchivedError();\\r\\n\\r\\n      const flagValues = { ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS };\\r\\n      for (const flag of ACCESS_FLAGS) {\\r\\n        flagValues[flag] = Boolean(profileRow?.[flag]);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      Object.assign(flagValues, withDerivedAccess(flagValues));\\r\\n      flagValues.is_admin = !!roleData;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Presence is owned by usePresenceHeartbeat; this read stays read-only.\\r\\n      // Writing last_seen_at here produced a write per permissions fetch and\\r\\n      // (with profiles in the realtime publication) a self-sustaining\\r\\n      // fetch -> write -> event -> fetch loop.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n      return flagValues;\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  // A dead session bounces to /auth once. Preserve lsatu_saved_email so\\r\\n  // re-login is smooth after an expiry bounce, and carry where they were: a\\r\\n  // bare \\"/auth\\" discarded the pathname, query, and hash, so a student whose\\r\\n  // token expired on an assignment link lost the link as well as the session.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (query.error instanceof AccountArchivedError) {\\r\\n      (async () => {\\r\\n        await signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true });\\r\\n        navigate(\\"/auth\\", { replace: true, state: { reason: \\"account_archived\\" } });\\r\\n      })();\\r\\n    } else if (query.error instanceof SessionExpiredError) {\\r\\n      const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\\r\\n      (async () => {\\r\\n        await signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true });\\r\\n        navigate(authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo), {\\r\\n          replace: true,\\r\\n          state: { reason: \\"session_expired\\" },\\r\\n        });\\r\\n      })();\\r\\n    } else if (query.error) {\\r\\n      console.error(\\"Failed to fetch permissions\\", query.error);\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    // `location` is intentionally not a dependency: this must capture where the\\r\\n    // user was when the session died, not follow them as they navigate.\\r\\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\\r\\n  }, [query.error, signOut, navigate]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS,\\r\\n    ...(query.data ?? {}),\\r\\n    // Only the true cold load (no cached data) blocks; cached reads and\\r\\n    // background refetches keep loading false, so gated navigations don\'t flash.\\r\\n    loading: authReady && !!user && query.isPending,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}}}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"i":2,"result":{"status":"fulfilled","value":{"chunk_id":"1e6f8f","wall_time_seconds":1.5157049,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":363,"output":"import type { ReactNode } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \'@/hooks/useUserPermissions\';\\r\\nimport { useReadingCenterAccess } from \'@/hooks/useReadingCenterAccess\';\\r\\nimport ProtectedRoute from \'@/components/ProtectedRoute\';\\r\\nimport LockedModule from \'@/components/LockedModule\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The list opens for Classroom access or a verified Reading Center grant.\\r\\n * MyWorkPage narrows the latter account to that one tool. Notes use their own gate. */\\r\\nexport default function MyWorkGate({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const generalAccess = permissions.is_admin || permissions.has_classroom_access;\\r\\n  const readingCenter = useReadingCenterAccess(!generalAccess);\\r\\n  if (!authReady || permissions.loading || (!!user && !generalAccess && readingCenter.isPending)) {\\r\\n    return <div role=\\"status\\" className=\\"student-destination-message\\">Checking assigned work access…</div>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return <ProtectedRoute allowSignedIn>\\r\\n    {generalAccess || readingCenter.allowed ? children : readingCenter.isError\\r\\n      ? <div role=\\"alert\\" className=\\"student-destination-message\\">We could not verify access to assigned work. <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"classroom-button\\" onClick={() => void readingCenter.refetch()}>Try again</button></div>\\r\\n      : <LockedModule />}\\r\\n  </ProtectedRoute>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}}}'}]
